HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1456d4ddf3194a92a4ff","text":"The later `الأرض كيف سطحت` made this furnishing unexpectedly cosmological: spread carpets and spread earth echo one another. `بث` B002 also made the garden an answer to concealed grief, especially after the painful first half.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2f42b230a4e179388125","text":"2. **Material display communicates what the quiet realm does not say aloud.** **Lexical:** `ب ث ث` B002, \"disclosing what is hidden in the self,\" activated after physical spreading B001. **Cue:** no vain voice is heard in 88:11, yet the carpets visibly disclose generosity; 88:21 later commands explicit reminding. **Role:** the spread objects act as nonverbal disclosure, while the later reminder turns disclosure into articulated cognition. **Abduction:** I infer communicative intent from arrangement; the packet describes objects, not a sender's stated emotion. **Reading change:** the carpets are a silent broadcast of welcome and care.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_359fe2ab649a800d8dd7","text":"1. **Carpets spread as generous dispersion.** Evidence: `ب ث ث` B001 spreading/scattering a thing. Structural cue: after rows of cushions, carpets are spread out. Role: `مبثوثة` extends comfort over the ground surface. Abductive move: the garden’s order includes both rowed arrangement and broad distribution. Reading change: the ayah reads as abundant prepared surface, not clutter.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B002","root_000697/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4ac61a9a9659c96e120e","text":"2. **Hidden grief replaced by disclosed ease.** Evidence: `ب ث ث` B002 disclosing hidden distress/grief; earlier `س ر ر` B001 hidden inward matter and B010 joy. Structural cue: inner states are repeatedly materialized in the garden. Role: what is “spread out” can be the release of what was hidden, but here as ease rather than complaint. Abductive move: the garden externalizes interior relief by spreading it underfoot. Reading change: `مبثوثة` carries affective disclosure, not only spatial spreading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5dffe30ceb6d86fb8b4d","text":"1. **Scattering here maximizes coverage rather than producing disorder.** **Lexical:** `ب ث ث` B001, \"spreading a thing out,\" including visible distribution of goods. **Cue:** spread carpets follow raised couches, placed cups, and rowed cushions in 88:13-15. **Role:** rows provide fixed local order, while broad dispersal extends usable comfort into the intervals and across the whole area. **Abduction:** I infer complementary design between alignment and scattering. **Reading change:** `مبثوثة` reads as deliberate saturation of space with access, not random mess.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_73f5dbd171775a98697f","text":"88:17-20 changed physical scattering into epistemic distribution: camel, sky, mountains, and earth became evidence spread across unlike domains. 88:21 then made the communicative function explicit as reminder, strongly activating `ب ث ث` B002-B003 disclosure and investigation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7589bea5436f26243131","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:16:2\",\"root_000083\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yayma aynı zamanda araştırıp açığa çıkarmayı çağrıştırarak serilmiş nesnelerden yaratılışın farklı alanlarına dağılmış kanıtları incelemeye geçiş kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_79218701ee2ad848a070","text":"3. **The visual spread becomes a training ground for investigation.** **Lexical:** `ب ث ث` B003, \"investigating and exposing a matter.\" **Cue:** immediately after the spread interior, 88:17-20 repeatedly directs sight to camel, sky, mountains, and earth and asks `كَيْفَ`; 88:21 names the result as reminder. **Role:** B003 moves from exposing objects across a surface to exposing the constructional question behind objects. **Abduction:** I infer that the furnishing sequence trains the same inspecting gaze later redirected to creation. **Reading change:** `مبثوثة` marks a hinge from distributed things to distributed evidence: the world itself will now be read as an exposed field for inquiry.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_92a6cc5d6a4bf5bfb366","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:16:2\",\"root_000083\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yayma, içtekini açığa vurur gibi, boş sözün duyulmadığı bahçede cömertlik ve karşılamayı maddi düzenle sessizce duyurur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000083/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b83dcfbc4f891fb7e961","text":"3. **Ground reversal before the flattened earth.** Evidence: `ب ث ث` B001 spreading; later `س ط ح` B001 flat extended surface and `ء ر ض` B001 lower ground. Structural cue: carpets are spread before the command to look at earth spread flat. Role: garden carpets give a cultivated version of earth’s spreadability.
